Motivation: CLIP-based CIL中，分类被分解为属性提取和属性聚合两个阶段，但增量学习只使用当前任务数据，导致提取和聚合都偏向新类，引发灾难性遗忘。

Method: 1) 属性提取稳定性：通过主测地线分析(PGA)将类级视觉和文本属性锚定在超球面嵌入空间；2) 属性聚合稳定性：学习轻量级任务特定专家，结合评分与残差精炼，并用变分信息瓶颈正则化；3) 推理：通过最优传输在任务属性流形上进行路由选择。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上一致超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将CLIP-based CIL分解为属性提取与聚合两个阶段，并分别设计稳定化策略，能有效缓解灾难性遗忘，提升增量学习性能。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) is important in building real-world learning systems. In CLIP-based CIL, the model performs classification by comparing similarity between visual and textual embeddings obtained from template prompts, e.g., ``a photo of a [CLASS]''. This seemingly monolithic matching process can be decomposed into two conceptually distinct stages: attribute extraction and attribute aggregation. For example, a model may recognize cat using attributes such as fur texture and whiskers. When learning a new class like car, the model must extract additional attributes like wheels and adjust how they are aggregated in the shared representation space. However, since only data from the current task is available, incremental updates can bias both attribute extraction and aggregation toward new classes, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Therefore, we propose AREA for attribute extraction and aggregation in CLIP-based CIL. To stabilize extraction, we anchor class-level visual and textual attributes on the hyperspherical embedding space via principal geodesic analysis. To stabilize aggregation, we learn lightweight task-specific experts with scoring and residual refinement, regularized by a variational information bottleneck objective. During inference, we perform routing over task attribute manifolds via optimal transport for more concise prediction. Experiments show that AREA consistently outperforms SOTA methods. Code is available at https://github.com/LAMDA-CL/ICML2026-AREA.

</details>


### [110] [HarmoVid: Relightful Video Portrait Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28811)
*Jun Myeong Choi,Jae Shin Yoon,Luchao Qi,Roni Sengupta,Joon-Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种视频光照协调方法，通过去闪烁模型和视频扩散模型，实现前景视频与背景场景在阴影、色调和光照强度上的自然匹配，解决了逐帧处理导致的时序抖动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频光照协调缺乏成对标注数据（不同光照下相同运动的视频），现有的图像方法逐帧处理会产生严重的时序闪烁伪影。

Method: 1) 提出光照去闪烁模型稳定全局和局部闪烁伪影；2) 视频扩散模型使用升级后的去闪烁数据（真实+合成视频）进行训练；3) 提出非对称alpha掩码条件技术学习真实视频中的干净边界。

Result: 模型在时序连贯性、自然度、边界清晰度和物理合理的光照行为上表现优异，同时保持较强的重光照表现力。

Conclusion: 相比现有的基于图像和基于视频的协调方法，该方法在多项指标上均取得更好效果。

Abstract: We present a method for harmonizing the lighting of a foreground video to match a target background scene, adjusting shadows, color tone, and illumination intensity (relightful harmonization). Unlike images, acquiring labeled data for videos, where identical motions are recorded under different lighting conditions, is practically infeasible and non-scalable. While one way to create such paired data is to apply existing image-based harmonization models frame by frame to a video, the resulting outputs often suffer from significant temporal jitters. We overcome this problem by introducing a novel lighting deflickering model that can stabilize the global and local lighting flickering artifacts. Our video diffusion model learns from these upgraded deflickered data with a volume of real and synthetic videos to generate high-quality video harmonization results. We further propose an asymmetric alpha mask conditioning technique to learn the clean boundaries from real videos. Experiments demonstrate that our model achieves strong temporal coherence, naturalness, cleaner boundaries, and physically meaningful lighting behavior, while maintaining strong relighting expressiveness compared to prior image-based and video-based harmonization methods.

</details>


### [111] [Gamma-World: Generative Multi-Agent World Modeling Beyond Two Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28816)
*Fangfu Liu,Kai He,Tianchang Shen,Tianshi Cao,Sanja Fidler,Yueqi Duan,Jun Gao,Igor Gilitschenski,Zian Wang,Xuanchi Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多智能体交互式视频生成的世界模型，通过Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding（无需学习的智能体表征）和Sparse Hub Attention（线性复杂度跨智能体注意力）实现可扩展的多智能体建模，并利用教师-学生蒸馏实现24FPS实时生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频生成的世界模型主要针对单智能体场景，无法处理多智能体同时交互的情况。多智能体设置需要智能体独立可控、排列对称且高效推理，而现有方法依赖学习的槽位身份或固定排序，扩展性差且计算成本高。

Method: 1) Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding：将3D RoPE扩展，使各智能体在旋转角度空间中占据正则单形的不同顶点，实现排列等价且无需额外参数；2) Sparse Hub Attention：引入可学习的枢纽令牌中介跨智能体交互，将交叉注意力复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N)；3) 教师-学生蒸馏：将全上下文扩散教师模型蒸馏为因果学生模型，支持KV缓存和时序块生成，实现24FPS实时交互。

Result: 在多玩家虚拟环境实验中，模型在视频保真度、动作可控性和智能体间一致性上优于基于槽位和密集注意力的基线方法，且能从两玩家泛化到四玩家而无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过精巧的归纳偏置设计（旋转编码与稀疏注意力），可以在不增加学习参数和计算复杂度的情况下，有效将世界模型扩展到多智能体交互场景，并为实时生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World models for interactive video generation have largely focused on single-agent settings, where future observations are generated from a single control signal. However, many generated environments require multi-agent interaction: multiple players, robots, or embodied agents act simultaneously within a shared space. Scaling world models to such settings requires a principled multi-agent design: agents should remain independently controllable, permutation-symmetric, and support efficient inference while maintaining consistency across time and perspectives. In this paper, we present our generative multi-agent world model for interactive simulation. It introduces Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding, a parameter-free extension of 3D RoPE that represents agents as vertices of a regular simplex in rotary angle space. This gives each agent a distinct phase while making all agents permutation-equivalent, enabling scalable agent identity without learned per-slot identities or a fixed agent ordering. To avoid dense all-to-all attention across agents, we further propose Sparse Hub Attention, where learnable hub tokens mediate token interaction across agents, reducing cross-agent attention cost from quadratic to linear in the number of agents. For real-time rollout, we distill a full-context diffusion teacher into a causal student that generates temporal blocks sequentially with KV caching, enabling action-responsive generation at 24 FPS. Experiments in multiplayer virtual environments show that our model improves video fidelity, action controllability, and inter-agent consistency over slot-based and dense-attention baselines, while generalizing from two to four players without additional training.

</details>


### [112] [From Pixels to Words -- Towards Native One-Vision Models at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28820)
*Haiwen Diao,Jiahao Wang,Penghao Wu,Yuhao Dong,Yuwei Niu,Yue Zhu,Zhongang Cai,Weichen Fan,Linjun Dai,Silei Wu,Xuanyu Zheng,Mingxuan Li,Yuanhan Zhang,Bo Li,Hanming Deng,Huchuan Lu,Quan Wang,Lei Yang,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NEO-ov是一个原生基础模型，无需外部编码器或适配器，通过端到端学习跨帧和像素-词对应关系，实现了细粒度的统一时空建模，在缩小与模块化VLM差距的同时，在细粒度视觉感知上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM通常采用模块化拼接方式（独立图像编码器+语言解码器），导致像素级信号在帧间碎片化，早期像素-词交互分散。而原生VLM在多图像、视频理解和空间智能方面探索不足。

Method: 提出NEO-ov原生基础模型，消除模块边界，端到端学习跨帧和像素-词对应关系，无需外部编码器、辅助适配器或事后融合，实现原生统一的时空建模。

Result: NEO-ov大幅缩小了与模块化VLM的差距，在细粒度视觉感知任务上表现优异，验证了原生"one-vision"架构在大规模下既可行又具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 原生非模块化VLM架构是可行的且有竞争力的方向；论文还提供了系统的架构分析和详细训练食谱，以促进后续原生多模态建模研究。

Abstract: Current vision-language models (VLMs) typically stitch together separate image encoders and language decoders via multi-stage alignment, a modular framework that inevitably fragments pixel-level signals across frames and scatters early pixel-word interactions. In parallel, native VLMs, despite impressive performance on single images, remain largely unexplored in multi-image, video understanding, and spatial intelligence. Hence, we introduce NEO-ov, a native foundation model that learns cross-frame and pixel-word correspondence end-to-end, without any external encoders, auxiliary adapters, or post-hoc fusion. By eliminating module boundaries entirely, NEO-ov enables fine-grained and unified spatiotemporal modeling to emerge natively inside the model. Notably, NEO-ov largely narrows the gap to modular counterparts while excelling at fine-grained visual perception, validating that native "one-vision" architectures are not only feasible but competitive at scale. Beyond empirical performance, we unveil systematic architectural analyses and detailed training recipes to facilitate subsequent native multimodal modeling. Our code and models are publicly available at: https://github.com/EvolvingLMMs-Lab/NEO.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [A Factory-Floor Deployment Case Study of VLA Pipelines for Industrial Packaging Task: Workflow, Failures, and Lessons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27461)
*Brian Zhu,Philipp Schmitt,Philine Meister,Lukas Gensler,Momen Khalil,Emmanuele Poggi,Johannes Hechtl,Carsten Braunroth,Kai Wurm,Gokul Narayanan,Eugen Solowjow,Georg von Wichert,Andre Scholz,Felix Albrecht,Maxmillian Metzner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在西门子工厂中，通过迭代微调Pi0.5策略，将VLA策略部署到实际工业包装任务（从杂乱堆中拾取透明配件袋并插入纸箱）的实践经验，记录了2535个episode的部署数据，总结了常见故障模式和改进工作流的经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在实验室环境中表现出色，但在真实工厂部署中面临可靠性挑战。作者希望通过实际的工厂部署研究，了解将预训练策略适配到具体工业任务所需的实际工作量，并总结可推广的经验教训。

Method: 采用迭代微调与部署驱动精炼的流程，包括数据收集、数据整理、微调、评估和针对性恢复数据收集的重复循环。在西门子工厂现场收集了2535个episode（10小时）的数据，对Pi0.5策略进行持续优化。

Result: 完成了一个实际的工业包装任务部署，积累了2535个episode的现场数据，识别出多个重复出现的故障模式，并总结出改进部署工作流的具体经验教训。

Conclusion: VLA策略在真实工厂部署是可行的，但需要大量迭代微调和针对性数据收集来应对实际环境中的故障模式。本文提供了宝贵的实证经验，为未来VLA在工业场景的落地提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown promising manipulation capabilities, yet their practical impact is often limited by the reliability demands of real-world deployment. We present a deployment study of an industrial packaging task at Siemens Factory (GWE, Erlangen, Germany), where a robot must pick a transparent accessory bag from a cluttered pile, insert it into the remaining cavity of a cardboard package, and ensure that the bag and its contents remain below the closing plane. Our goal is to understand the practical effort required to adapt a pretrained Pi0.5 policy to a single factory-floor task through iterative fine-tuning and deployment-driven refinement. The pipeline consists of repeated loops of data collection, curation, fine-tuning, evaluation, and targeted recovery data collection. We have accumulated 2535 episodes (10 hours) from the on-site factory settings. In this paper, we contribute an empirical account of a factory-floor VLA deployment, highlighting recurring failure modes and lessons that inform how to improve the deployment workflow.

</details>


### [114] [GE-Sim 2.0: A Roadmap Towards Comprehensive Closed-loop Video World Simulators for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27491)
*Boxiang Qiu,Liliang Chen,Yue Liao,Nan Wang,Lintao Wang,Jiayi Luo,Wenzhi Zhao,Shengcong Chen,Di Chen,Ye Li,Chen Gao,Shuicheng Yan,Si Liu,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GE-Sim 2.0是一个面向机器人操作的闭环视频世界模拟器，通过三个新模块（状态专家、世界判断器、加速框架）实现了从视频仿真到策略学习的闭环，在仅2B参数下登顶WorldArena榜单，并验证了仿真训练的policy可迁移到真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型在动作跟随保真度、轨迹覆盖范围以及从视频仿真到策略学习的闭环能力上存在不足，缺乏可扩展的评估和闭环学习平台。

Method: 基于Genie Envisioner的动作条件视频生成框架，在数千小时真实机器人数据上重新训练；提出三个闭环模块：1) 状态专家从视频潜在变量解码本体感知状态；2) 世界判断器对生成轨迹进行任务评分；3) 加速框架实现单卡H100上2.3秒生成25帧并支持推理时4倍跳帧。

Result: 仅2B参数登顶WorldArena公开榜单，超越专用机器人世界模型和闭源通用视频生成器；基于其生成轨迹和奖励训练的策略在真实场景中有可量化的提升。

Conclusion: GE-Sim 2.0是一个实用的可扩展评估与闭环操作策略学习平台，验证了仿真训练到真实迁移的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce GE-Sim 2.0 (Genie Envisioner World Simulator 2.0), a closed-loop video world simulator for robotic manipulation. Building on the action-conditioned video generation framework of Genie Envisioner, GE-Sim 2.0 is re-trained on thousands of hours of real-world robot data spanning teleoperation, contact-rich interaction, and on-robot policy deployment, substantially improving action-following fidelity and trajectory coverage. On top of this foundation, three new modules close the loop from video simulation to policy learning: a state expert that decodes proprioceptive state from video latents to support next-chunk prediction by downstream VLA policies; a world judge that scores generated rollouts against task instructions, yielding machine-verifiable success signals and rewards in place of manual inspection; and an acceleration framework that delivers a 25-frame rollout in 2.3 seconds on a single H100, with up to 4* frame skipping at inference for long-horizon evaluation. GE-Sim 2.0 tops the public WorldArena leaderboard at only 2B parameters, outperforming both dedicated robotic world models and closed-source general video generators, and policies trained against its rollouts and rewards translate into measurable real-world gains, establishing GE-Sim 2.0 as a practical platform for scalable evaluation and closed-loop learning of manipulation policies.

</details>


### [115] [SCALE-COMM: Shared, Contrastively-Aligned Latent Embeddings for MARL Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27532)
*Mahmoud Abouelyazid,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCALE-COMM框架，通过对比对齐的潜在嵌入解耦通信学习与策略优化，提升多机器人协同中通信表征的稳定性与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涌现通信方法存在通信协议不稳定、消息语义无根基、通信学习与策略优化相互干扰等问题，导致协同性能随时间退化。

Method: SCALE-COMM：自监督框架，学习低维潜在消息解耦通信与策略优化，通过对比对齐确保跨智能体与时间步的一致性。

Result: 在标准MARL基准和仓库协调任务上，SCALE-COMM在表征质量和任务性能上均优于现有通信框架，展现出更好的稳定性、样本效率和吞吐量。

Conclusion: 表征驱动的通信方法能够有效提升多智能体协同的可扩展性与鲁棒性，通信表征质量是影响协同性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Emergent communication enables partially observant Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) to coordinate effectively in decentralized multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) settings. However, existing approaches often struggle with unstable communication protocols, ungrounded message semantics, and interference between communication learning and policy optimization, leading to degraded coordination over time. We propose SCALE-COMM (Shared, Contrastively-Aligned Latent Embeddings for COMMunication), a self-supervised framework for learning compact, stable, and policy-relevant communication representations. SCALE-COMM decouples communication learning from policy optimization by training low-dimensional latent messages that capture task-relevant planning and traffic information, while enforcing consistency across agents and time. Across standard MARL benchmarks and a realistic warehouse coordination task, SCALE-COMM consistently outperforms existing communication frameworks in both representation quality and task performance. The learned communication space yields improved stability, sample efficiency, and throughput under policy fine-tuning, demonstrating the effectiveness of representation-driven communication for scalable multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [116] [Inducing Calmness With Pocket-Sized Robotics: Reducing Movement and Heart Rate in Children through Hand-Held Tactile Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27533)
*Morten Roed Frederiksen,Kasper Støy,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探究手持触觉设备（通过振动匹配游戏）对典型发育儿童生理与行为镇静指标的影响。18名儿童参与被试内实验，结果显示触觉交互使心率降低3.56 bpm（p<0.01），整体运动减少38%（p<0.05），注意力相关身体区域静止度提升45%。表明短时触觉游戏可下调生理激活，促进镇静与专注状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童在高度唤醒或躁动状态下难以专注、自我调节和身体镇静。现有技术缺乏简单易得的触觉交互手段来促进具身自我调节，因此本文探索手持触觉设备对儿童平静状态的影响。

Method: 采用被试内设计，18名典型发育儿童参与两种条件（有/无触觉交互）。使用手持振动匹配游戏设备，同步记录心率和全身运动，对比两种条件下的生理唤醒和身体躁动水平。

Result: 触觉游戏交互使心率显著降低3.56 bpm（p<0.01）；整体运动减少38%（p<0.05）；注意力相关身体区域（如头部、躯干）运动减少达45%。

Conclusion: 短暂的触觉游戏式手持设备交互能够下调生理激活（心率）和身体躁动（运动幅度），有效促进儿童的镇静和专注状态，为注意力和行为调节提供支持。

Abstract: Periods of heightened arousal or restlessness can interfere with children's ability to focus, self-regulation, and physically calm. Technologies that encourage embodied self-regulation through tactile interaction may provide a simple and accessible means of promoting calmness. This paper investigates how interaction with a pocket-sized tactile device influences physiological and behavioral markers of calmness in typically developing children. Building on prior work examining heart rate modulation, we present new findings on how tactile interaction affects full-body movement and postural stability. We employ a device that engages children through a hand-held rhythmic vibration-matching game, designed to focus attention and encourage stillness. Eighteen children participated in a within-subjects study that involved two conditions: with and without tactile interaction with a hand-held device, while having their heart rate and body movement recorded. Results show that the tactile game interaction reduced physiological arousal (heart rate decreased by 3.56 bpm, p < 0.01) and physical restlessness (overall movement decreased by 38%, p < 0.05), with attention-related body regions showing the greatest change toward stillness (45% reduction in movement). These findings demonstrate that brief tactile game-like engagement with a hand-held device can down-regulate physiological activation, promoting the calm and focused states toward sustained attention and behavior regulation.

</details>


### [117] [Synthetic Emotions vs. Gamification: Exploring Engagement Strategies for Small Social Robots in Different Age Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27539)
*Morten Roed Frederiksen,Kasper Støy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了触觉机器人在日常互动中支持焦虑症儿童的参与策略，比较了情感反馈和积分奖励两种方式。通过对6-8岁儿童（偏好情感反馈）和20-27岁大学生（积分系统带来更高任务准确率）的两项研究发现，不同年龄群体的偏好与实际行为结果存在差异，强调了通过观察验证设计假设的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多儿童在情绪调节和社交互动方面存在困难，限制了他们在日常活动和治疗项目中的参与。为了让社交辅助机器人有效支持这些儿童，需要确保他们持续且有意义的参与。本文旨在探索触觉机器人中有效的参与策略。

Method: 设计了两种参与策略：合成情感反馈和积分奖励。进行了两项研究：1）对16名6-8岁学龄儿童的偏好评估；2）对14名20-27岁大学生的全天自然行为研究。

Result: 学龄儿童更偏好情感反馈而非积分奖励；大学生在积分奖励条件下任务准确率显著更高（p < 0.05），且表现随时间保持稳定。

Conclusion: 不同用户群体的陈述偏好与实际行为结果存在差异，取决于参与环境；年龄差异会影响参与策略的有效性；需要通过观察互动来验证设计假设。

Abstract: Many children experience challenges in emotional regulation and social interaction, which can limit their participation in everyday activities and therapeutic programs. For socially assistive robots to be effective in this context, it is essential that children remain consistently and meaningfully engaged. We explore engagement strategies for a tactile robot designed to support children suffering from anxiety disorders through daily interactions. The robot delivers either synthetic emotional feedback or point rewards to encourage user participation. We evaluated these strategies through two studies: a preference assessment with 16 school children aged 6-8 years, and a behavioral study with 14 university students aged 20-27 years in naturalistic environments. The study with school children indicated a preference for emotional engagement over points-based approaches. The follow up study with university students across a full day of interactions revealed contrasting results: points-based systems produced significantly higher task accuracy (p < 0.05) and sustained performance over time. Findings from different user groups suggest that stated preferences and behavioral outcomes can diverge depending on engagement context, highlighting the importance of validating design assumptions through observed interaction. This work contributes insights into age-related differences in engagement strategy effectiveness in human-robot interaction design.

</details>


### [118] [Uni-LaViRA: Language-Vision-Robot Actions Translation for Unified Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27582)
*Hongyu Ding,Sizhuo Zhang,Ziming Xu,Jinwen Guo,Hongxiu Liu,Xingzhi Cheng,Zixuan Chen,Haifei Qi,Duo Wang,Hao Xu,Jieqi Shi,Yifan Zhang,Jing Huo,Jian Cheng,Yang Gao,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-LaViRA，一种无需训练的零样本具身导航架构，将语言-视觉-动作翻译统一为多模态大语言模型可直接输出的语义方向和像素目标，并引入TODO记忆列表与二次回溯机制，在多个导航任务和异构机器人上超越或匹敌需要大量数据训练的导航基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航方法依赖大规模机器人轨迹数据训练VLA基础模型，但作者认为导航的通用性可以通过结构性设计而非仅靠数据规模获得——导航的决策结构本质上是语言-视觉-动作翻译，其输出刚好落在预训练MLLM的自然输出流形内，因此可以借助推理而非机器人数据学习来完成。

Method: 提出Uni-LaViRA统一智能体架构：（1）将导航任务分解为语言动作（输出语义级方向指令）和视觉动作（输出像素级视觉目标），两者均利用预训练MLLM的已有能力；（2）引入TODO列表记忆（TDM），每一步维护结构化的待办子目标清单并回注到注意力窗口；（3）引入二次回溯（SCB），检测到错误后回滚至前一状态，将失败子轨迹作为条件重新规划。

Result: 零训练条件下，在VLN-CE R2R上达60.7% SR，VLN-CE RxR 51.3%，HM3D-v2 77.7%，HM3D-OVON 60.0%，MP3D-EQA 54.7%，OpenUAV 40.0%，在四个任务族和四种异构真实机器人（轮式、四足、人形、自建无人机）上均实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 导航的通用性可以通过结构设计而非数据规模获得；预训练MLLM天然具备导航所需的语义方向与视觉目标输出能力；TDM和SCB两个智能体循环机制使得统一架构在多种任务和异构机器人上零样本可行，匹配甚至超越依赖百万级样本和数千GPU小时训练的导航基础模型。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires an agent to map language and visual observations to a stream of spatial actions that drive a real robot through environments it has never seen. The dominant approach has been to scale vision-language-action (VLA) foundation models on ever-larger collections of robot trajectories. This paper argues that, for navigation specifically, generality can be obtained structurally, not only through data scale. The underlying decision structure of navigation reduces to a single Language-Vision-Robot Actions Translation. The language action emits semantic-level directional command and the vision action emits a pixel-level visual target. Both outputs lie inside the natural output manifold of pretrained multimodal large language models (MLLMs), so the task can be reasoned about by an agent rather than learned from robot data. Therefore, we present Uni-LaViRA, a unified agentic architecture that extends the same insight to four task families (VLN-CE, ObjectNav, EQA, and Aerial-VLN) and to four heterogeneous real robots (Wheeled, Quadruped, Humanoid robot, and a self-built UAV) in a zero-shot manner. Two agent-loop mechanisms make this unification practical. TODO List Memory (TDM) rewrites a structured checklist of pending sub-goals at every step, reciting the unfinished items back into the agent's most recent attention window. Second Chance Backtrack (SCB) rolls the robot back to the pre-error state and conditions the agent's next plan on the failed sub-trajectory, turning single-pass navigation into a self-correcting process. With zero training effort, Uni-LaViRA reaches 60.7% SR on VLN-CE R2R, 51.3% on VLN-CE RxR, 77.7% on HM3D-v2, 60.0% on HM3D-OVON, 54.7% on MP3D-EQA, and 40.0% on OpenUAV, matching or even surpassing recent training navigation foundation models that consume millions of samples and thousands of GPU-hours.

</details>


### [119] [Agentic Language-to-Objective Synthesis for Optofluidic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27643)
*Ivan Saraev,Elena Erben,Weida Liao,Fan Nan,Gerhard Neumann,Eric Lauga,Moritz Kreysing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Speak-to-Objective框架，利用大语言模型将自然语言命令转化为可微目标函数，结合光流控实验平台实现可编程微粒子组装，构建了从意图到执行的自动化闭环流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光制造和微机器人领域缺乏将用户意图自动转化为可执行数学目标的工具，目标函数的手工设计繁琐、难以复用，制约了可编程闭环制造的发展。

Method: 提出Speak-to-Objective模块化流水线，采用感知→组合→提议→执行→报告与学习的紧凑循环，利用条件化LLM将语音/文字指令翻译为可微目标函数，并通过SLSQP逆求解器在光流控平台上执行微粒子组装。

Result: 该方法能组合几何、间距和拓扑项生成鲁棒描述性目标，实现从部分轨迹恢复组装、抵抗扰动，以及精确放置，在激光诱导热粘性流驱动的微流控环境中验证了自然语言可编程的微尺度组装。

Conclusion: 本工作证明了自然语言、可微目标函数和激光驱动可耦合为可复用数字工作流，为自驱动、AI辅助的光学制造平台奠定了基础。

Abstract: Light-based advanced manufacturing increasingly requires programmable, closed-loop tools that translate human design intent into executable operations at small length scales. Yet a key bottleneck persists across robotic and manufacturing modalities: turning user intent into machine-readable objectives that are reliably executable. While micro-robotics offers versatile manipulation via optical actuation of fluids, mathematically tractable goal specification remains manual and hard to reuse. Here, we introduce Speak-to-Objective, a modular agentic pipeline that uses a conditioned Large Language Model (LLM) to translate spoken or written commands into fully differentiable objective functions for assembling microparticles in a constraint-aware inverse solver (SLSQP) and on an experimental optofluidic platform. The approach employs a compact loop - perceive -> compose -> propose -> act -> report & learn - that treats the objective as the interface between intent and actuation, separating what to assemble or pattern from how to actuate, while learning from user feedback. The pipeline composes geometry, spacing, and assignment/topology terms to generate robust descriptive objectives that assemble from partial traces and recover after perturbations, as well as explicit objectives for precise placement, all in an actuator-agnostic fashion. Using laser-induced thermoviscous flows as the physical actuation modality, we demonstrate natural-language-programmable, light-based microscale assembly of particle patterns in a microfluidic environment. Beyond its immediate impact on programmable microassembly, and using laser-induced optofluidic actuation as a reduced-complexity experimental platform, our work points toward self-driving, AI-assisted optical manufacturing platforms in which natural language, differentiable objectives, and laser-based actuation are coupled into a reusable digital workflow.

</details>


### [120] [Trinity: Unifying Class-Agnostic Terrain and Semantic Segmentation for Unstructured Outdoor Environments by Leveraging Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27644)
*Marcus G Müller,Wout Boerdijk,Maximilian Durner,Riccardo Giubilato,Abel Gawel,Wolfgang Stürzl,Roland Siegwart,Rudolph Triebel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Trinity——统一transformer网络，联合执行类特定语义分割和类无关地形分割，在合成和真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的地形可通过性估计方法依赖机器人特定标注或语义类别映射，跨平台迁移性差，且标准语义分割仅关注预定义类别，无法覆盖多样化地形。

Method: 提出Trinity网络，基于transformer架构，统一框架内同时进行类特定语义分割和类无关地形分割；扩展OAISYS模拟器，构建RUGDSynth合成数据集，并推出EXTerra真实数据集。

Result: 实验证明了联合分割方法在复杂户外环境中的有效性，验证了所提任务的可行性。

Conclusion: Trinity通过联合学习类特定与类无关的地形分割，实现了机器人无关的视觉地形先验，可迁移至多种下游任务，并提供了配套数据集。

Abstract: Terrain understanding is fundamental for mobile robots operating in unstructured outdoor environments. Existing vision-based traversability estimation methods rely on robot-specific annotations or semantic class mappings, limiting transferability across platforms and requiring costly re-annotation when robot capabilities change, while standard semantic segmentation methods only focus on specific predefined classes, which do not capture the variety of terrains. In this work, we propose a transformer-based architecture that jointly performs class-specific semantic segmentation and class-agnostic terrain segmentation within a unified network, called Trinity. Terrain regions are segmented based solely on visual appearance, without predefined semantic labels or robot-dependent traversability scores. This formulation enables the learning of robot-agnostic visual terrain priors that can be combined with robot-specific experience for downstream tasks such as traversability estimation, visual odometry, and mission planning. To enable large-scale training with diverse terrain appearances, we extend the OAISYS simulator and introduce RUGDSynth, a synthetic dataset inspired by RUGD with class-agnostic terrain samples. Furthermore, we present the EXTerra Dataset, providing real-world images annotated with both class-specific and class-agnostic terrain labels. Experiments demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed task and the effectiveness of our joint segmentation approach in complex outdoor environments. Code and datasets will be released with this publication (after review).

</details>


### [121] [Design of a Real-time Asynchronous Monocular Odometry for Planetary Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27661)
*Benat Inigo,Florian Steidle,Wolfgang Stuerzl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件相机的实时异步单目里程计系统，利用Error-State Kalman Filter（ESKF）结合异步特征追踪器RATE，在严格计算约束下（如行星探测）实现高鲁棒性的相机自我运动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星探测车在复杂、不可预测的环境中需要高速感知和高动态范围（HDR）鲁棒性，传统相机难以满足这些需求。事件相机通过异步报告像素级亮度变化，以微秒级分辨率工作，同时大幅降低数据带宽，从而解决极端光照条件下的感知问题。

Method: 基于Error-State Kalman Filter（ESKF），利用事件相机产生的异步事件流，结合实时异步特征追踪器RATE提供的跟踪位置输出，连续更新相机状态以估计自我运动。

Result: 论文为初步设计阶段，尚未给出具体实验数值结果，但描述了系统在严格计算约束下实现实时异步单目里程计的可行性。

Conclusion: 事件相机结合ESKF和异步特征追踪器，能够在计算资源受限的行星探测环境中实现鲁棒的实时里程计估计，特别适用于高动态范围场景。

Abstract: We describe our preliminary design of a real-time asynchronous event-based monocular odometry for planetary exploration. Operating under strict computational constraints, planetary rovers frequently encounter complex, unpredictable environments that demand high-speed sensing and robustness to high dynamic range (HDR) lighting. Event cameras address these needs by reporting asynchronous, pixel-wise brightness changes with microsecond resolution, significantly reducing data bandwidth while maintaining robustness in extreme lighting conditions. We propose an approach based on an Error-State Kalman Filter (ESKF) that leverages this asynchronous event stream to continuously estimate camera ego-motion. The camera state is updated with every tracked position output generated by RATE, a real-time asynchronous feature tracker.

</details>


### [122] [Simulation-Informed Diffusion for Decentralized Multi-robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27697)
*Jinhao Liang,Sven Koenig,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Simulation-Informed Diffusion (SID)框架，通过约束感知扩散模型模拟邻居未来轨迹，实现去中心化多机器人运动规划，在拥挤场景中显著优于基线方法，并扩展至108台机器人、160个障碍物的复杂环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化多机器人运动规划方法仅基于局部观测的静态快照生成轨迹，无法预测邻居机器人的未来行为，导致在机器人数量增多、环境拥挤时规划效果受限。

Method: 提出Simulation-Informed Diffusion (SID)框架，核心基于约束感知扩散模型(CADM)。首先利用CADM从当前观测状态模拟邻居的未来轨迹，再利用同一CADM在模拟信息的约束下规划自身轨迹。同时设计了仅在高拥挤场景下触发的极简通信方案。

Result: 在多种环境下的实验表明，SID在规划有效性和约束满足方面始终优于基线方法，并能扩展到108台机器人和160个障碍物的场景。

Conclusion: 通过将邻居轨迹模拟引入规划过程，SID有效解决了静态观测的局限性，在保持去中心化的同时提升了拥挤场景下的规划性能，且实现了最小化通信开销。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-robot motion planning requires each robot to generate collision-free trajectories from local observations, without global sensing or reliable communication. However, most existing planners, whether classical or learning-based, generate trajectories from a static snapshot of the local observation, which limits their ability to anticipate the future behavior of neighboring robots. This limitation is critical as the number of robots increases and the environment becomes more cluttered. To overcome this challenge, this paper introduces Simulation-Informed Diffusion (SID), a decentralized framework built on constraint-aware diffusion models (CADM). SID first uses CADM to simulate the future trajectories of neighboring robots from their currently observed states, and then uses the same CADM to plan each robot's own trajectory under safety constraints informed by these simulations. Crucially, the accurate simulation of neighbors enables a minimal communication scheme that triggers coordination only when necessary in highly congested scenarios. Experiments across diverse environments show that SID consistently outperforms baseline methods in terms of planning effectiveness and constraint satisfaction, and scales to scenarios with 108 robots and 160 obstacles.

</details>


### [123] [AURA: Asymptotically Optimal Uncertainty-Robust Replanning Algorithm for Kinodynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27699)
*Seyedali Golestaneh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Donghyung Lee,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为\method的在线运动规划框架，在执行过程中持续重规划和优化控制输入，以提高轨迹质量和跟踪精度，并处理运动不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于采样的运动规划器通常离线运行，执行前必须完成轨迹计算，且在有运动不确定性时无法准确跟踪规划轨迹，导致偏离名义解。本文旨在解决这些局限。

Method: 提出了一个统一的元规划框架\method，包含三个部分：主执行线程、持续探索状态空间并优化轨迹的重规划方法、以及优化未来控制输入以减少跟踪误差的优化过程。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的多个系统上进行了评估，与基线方法相比，在轨迹质量、跟踪精度和整体性能上均有一致的改进。

Conclusion: \method能够在线利用渐近最优规划，同时在不确定性下提高执行精度，是一种有效应对运动不确定性的在线运动规划框架。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planners offer a practical and scalable approach to kinodynamic motion planning, notably for high-dimensional, underactuated, or non-holonomic systems. However, these planners are typically used offline, requiring execution to begin only after the trajectory has been computed. In addition, the planned trajectory may not be accurately tracked in the presence of motion uncertainty, leading to deviations from the nominal solution. In this work, these limitations were addressed within a unified framework, \method, an asymptotically-optimal meta-planner framework that improves both path quality and tracking performance during execution. In addition to the main execution thread, this framework comprises a replanning method that continuously explores the state space and refines the trajectory during execution, and an optimization process that refines future control inputs to reduce tracking error. Together, these components enable \method to leverage asymptotically optimal planning online while improving execution accuracy under uncertainty. The proposed approach is evaluated in both simulation and real-world environments across multiple systems, demonstrating consistent improvements in trajectory quality, tracking accuracy, and overall performance compared with baseline methods.

</details>


### [124] [HumanoidMimicGen: Data Generation for Loco-Manipulation via Whole-Body Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27724)
*Kevin Lin,Ajay Mandlekar,Caelan Reed Garrett,Nikita Chernyadev,Yu Fang,Runyu Ding,Yuqi Xie,Justin Tran,Linxi Fan,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidMimicGen方法，通过少量源演示自动生成大量人形机器人全身 locomotion-manipulation 数据，显著提升模仿学习策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人模仿学习需要大量演示数据，但遥操作收集数据耗时费力；现有自动数据生成方法对高维全身动作空间（手臂、腿、躯干）的人形机器人效果不佳。

Method: 1) 从少量源演示中适配接触丰富的全身技能到新状态；2) 通过交错执行单/双臂技能与全身运动规划，生成稳定无碰撞的数据；3) 构建包含9个多样化任务的模拟基准进行系统评估。

Result: 在9种不同任务上，使用HumanoidMimicGen生成数据联合训练的全身视觉运动策略，相比仅使用真实数据训练的策略性能提升20%。

Conclusion: HumanoidMimicGen能有效自动生成大规模人形机器人locomotion-manipulation数据集，支持系统研究数据生成与策略学习决策对模型性能的影响。

Abstract: Imitation learning is a promising approach for training humanoid robots to both walk and manipulate, but it requires a large number of demonstrations, which are time-intensive and difficult to collect via teleoperation. Existing data-generation algorithms can automatically synthesize demonstrations for manipulators, but they are ineffective on humanoids because their high-dimensional composite action spaces involve arms, legs, and torsos. We present HumanoidMimicGen, a method for generating humanoid legged loco-manipulation data. Our method adapts contact-rich whole-body skills from a handful of source demonstrations to new states, generalizing across changes in object pose. By interleaving these single- and dual-arm skills with whole-body locomotion and manipulation planning, the method generates stable, collision-free data across diverse scenes and layouts. To evaluate our approach, we introduce a new simulated loco-manipulation benchmark containing nine diverse tasks that test humanoid loco-manipulation capabilities. There, we demonstrate that HumanoidMimicGen automatically generates large datasets for imitation learning and enables a systematic study of how data generation and policy learning decisions impact model performance. We show that whole-body visuomotor policies co-trained with data generated by HumanoidMimicGen outperform those trained only on real-world data by 20%.

</details>


### [125] [Colosseum V2: Benchmarking Generalization for Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27759)
*Jeremy Morgan,Prajwal Vijay,Hyeonho Oh,Jincen Song,Ashvin Arora,Alina Du,Gaurav Sukhatme,Jesse Thomason,Ishika Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Colosseum V2，一个大规模仿真基准，用于系统评估VLA模型在机器人操作中的泛化能力。该基准包含28个任务、13个类别和两种机器人形态，支持领域内和领域外测试。实验表明，现有方法（如ACT和Pi0.5）在基础性能和泛化方面均存在局限，且仿真与真实世界指标高度相关，验证了基准的生态效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA模型在视觉和语言预训练方面取得了进展，但其在分布偏移下任务性能常常下降，暴露出高层次理解转化为鲁棒行为之间的差距。现有缺乏一个能够系统研究这一差距的标准化基准。

Method: 基于ManiSkill模拟器构建Colosseum V2基准，包含28个任务（13个类别）、两种机器人形态，支持GPU并行化评估。在领域内和领域外条件下评估ACT和Pi0.5等方法。

Result: 现有方法（ACT、Pi0.5）在基础性能和泛化方面均表现不佳；仿真与真实世界指标之间存在强相关性，验证了基准的生态效度。

Conclusion: Colosseum V2通过标准化任务、指标和评估协议，实现了可重复、公平的比较，降低了评估开销，加速了通用机器人策略的研究进展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demonstrate promising generalization in robotic manipulation, driven by advances in large-scale vision and language pre-training. This progress can be misleading. Despite the zero-shot perception and language capabilities of VLAs, their overall task performance often degrades under distribution shifts, revealing gaps in how these systems translate high-level understanding into robust behavior. To systematically study this gap, we introduce Colosseum V2, a large-scale simulation benchmark for evaluating VLA generalization in robot learning across diverse conditions. The benchmark comprises 28 tasks spanning 13 task categories and two robot morphologies, covering a wide range of manipulation primitives and long-horizon behaviors. Built on the ManiSkill simulator, Colosseum V2 enables fast, GPU-parallelized evaluation and supports both in-domain and out-of-domain testing at scale. We evaluate state-of-the-art methods, including Action Chunking Transformers (ACT) and Pi0.5, and reveal limitations in both base performance and generalization. We demonstrate strong correlations between simulation and real-world metrics that support the ecological validity of the benchmark. By standardizing tasks, metrics, and evaluation protocols within a unified benchmark, Colosseum V2 enables reproducible and fair comparisons, reduced evaluation overhead, and accelerated progress toward general-purpose robot policies.

</details>


### [126] [Turning Video Models into Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27817)
*Sizhe Lester Li,Evan Kim,Xingjian Bai,Tong Zhao,Tao Pang,Max Simchowitz,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦的视频到动作机器人控制框架VERA：保留预训练视频生成模型不变，仅训练一个基于机器人雅可比矩阵的逆向动力学模型（IDM）来将生成的视频翻译为具体动作。该方法支持跨本体零样本迁移，在仿真和真实环境中均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要微调视频模型使其联合预测未来观测和动作，这需要大量带动作标签的数据，且破坏了视频模型的灵境无关性。作者希望探索一种解耦方案：保持视频规划器不变，只训练一个依赖于本体的逆向动力学模型来将视频翻译为动作，从而获得更好的数据效率、跨本体迁移能力和模块灵活性。

Method: VERA将控制流水线解耦为两部分：(1) 一个无动作的视频世界模型（video planner），用于生成未来观测的视频序列；(2) 一个基于机器人本体的雅可比矩阵设计的逆向动力学模型（IDM），用于从生成的视频中推断出具体的动作指令。IDM可以独立地使用自对弈数据训练，视频规划器无需任何微调，且不同本体只需更换对应的IDM即可。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界基准测试中表现优异，包括零样本Panda机械臂操作任务和16自由度Allegro手灵巧立方体重定向。同一视频规划器可通过配对不同的IDM在多个本体上使用，实现了跨本体的零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 解耦的视频规划加忠实视频到动作翻译（VERA）是迈向零样本、跨本体、可泛化机器人控制的一条可行替代路线，证明了无需微调视频生成模型也能高效实现机器人控制。

Abstract: Video generative models have emerged as a promising robotics backbone, capable of generating videos that depict the completion of complex tasks across embodiments and environments. Recent work proposes robot foundation models that jointly predict future observations and actions by finetuning video models with action-labeled data. In this paper, we test the limits of an alternative approach: leave the video planner as-is while training an embodiment-specific inverse dynamics model (IDM). This decoupling offers several natural benefits: the video planner remains embodiment-agnostic, different video models can be interchanged easily without re-training the IDM, and the IDM can be independently trained with readily available self-play data. We present a closed-loop, video-to-action policy that combines an action-free video world model with a carefully-designed IDM based on the robot embodiment Jacobian. We demonstrate that our IDM design is both data-efficient and scalable to high-dimensional action spaces. Our policy, which we coin the Video-to-Embodied Robot Action Model (VERA), achieves strong performance across simulated and real-world benchmarks, including zero-shot Panda arm manipulation and 16-DoF Allegro-hand dexterous cube re-orientation. The same video planner can be used across multiple embodiments by pairing it with different embodiment-specific IDMs. Our results show that decoupled video planning plus faithful video-to-action translation is a viable alternative route towards zero-shot, cross-embodiment, and generalizable robot control. More results are available on our project website: https://vera.csail.mit.edu.

</details>


### [127] [Tabero: Learning Gentle Manipulation with Closed-Loop Force Feedback from Vision, Touch, and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27886)
*Qiwei Wu,Rui Zhang,Xin Xiang,Tao Li,Weihua Zhang,Junjie Lai,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Tabero基准和模型套件，通过解耦力-位置指令接口的VTLA架构，利用数据高效管道生成触觉-视觉-语言任务，使机器人能在语言指令下实现轻柔操作，平均夹持力降低70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏触觉反馈进行轻柔操作的能力，主要因为对齐的视觉-触觉-语言数据稀缺，且缺少有效的闭环力反馈机制。

Method: 1) Tabero基准：数据高效管道，将开源操作轨迹重用于生成多种触觉-视觉-语言任务；多维评估协议衡量任务成功率和物理交互质量。2) Tabero-VTLA架构：解耦的力-位置命令接口，配合固定混合控制器实现实时力感知操作。

Result: 在高任务成功率下，轻柔指令的平均夹持力降低超过70%，证明模型能基于多模态经验调节交互力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦力-位置控制和数据高效管道，有效解决了触觉数据稀缺问题，实现了语言引导下的轻柔机器人操作。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for robots to achieve human-like gentle manipulation. However, existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models struggle to exploit tactile feedback for gentle manipulation due to scarce aligned vision-tactile-language data and the lack of effective closed-loop force feedback mechanisms. To address these challenges, we introduce Tabero, a benchmark and model suite for gentle, language-conditioned robotic manipulation that demands fine-grained contact force perception. First, the Tabero benchmark addresses the scarcity of tactile data by presenting a data-efficient pipeline that repurposes open-source robot manipulation trajectories to generate diverse vision-tactile-language tasks, and establishes a multidimensional evaluation protocol that measures task success alongside physical interaction quality. Second, we propose Tabero-VTLA, an architecture with a decoupled force-position command interface; the resulting force-position commands are executed by a fixed hybrid controller to enable real-time, force-aware manipulation. Evaluated on Tabero, our model maintains high task success while reducing average grip force by over 70\% under gentle instructions, demonstrating its ability to modulate interaction forces based on multimodal experience. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/NathanWu7/Tabero.

</details>


### [128] [S-Cheetah: A Novel Quadrupedal Robot with a 3-DOF Active Spine Learning Agile Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27909)
*Zimu Li,Weibang Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出S-Cheetah，一款搭载3-DOF仿生脊柱的 quadruped 机器人，结合强化学习框架（加速度课程学习+专项奖励函数），实现6.9 m/s疾驰、7.2 rad/s原地转向及空中自翻转正能力，证明多自由度脊柱对四足机器人机动性的全面提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人脊柱研究为降低控制复杂性，多减少脊柱自由度，无法实现生物脊柱的空间三轴旋转。如何在多自由度仿生脊柱上有效发挥其优势以提升四足机器人敏捷运动能力，是当前研究的重要挑战。

Method: 设计3-DOF仿生串联主动脊柱（支持三轴旋转）；开发专用强化学习框架，包含加速度课程学习策略以及疾驰步态奖励、脊柱波动奖励、脊柱转向奖励等定制化奖励函数，主动促进脊柱参与并最大化运动能力。

Result: 旋转G2疾驰步态下峰值速度达6.9 m/s，原地转向角速度7.2 rad/s；展示出猫科动物启发的空中自翻转能力，能从任意自由落体姿态稳定四足着地。

Conclusion: 通过多种运动任务的全面评估，证明3-DOF脊柱的引入整体提升了四足机器人的运动敏捷性。

Abstract: The biological spine of quadrupeds enables sagittal flexion/extension, lateral bending, and axial rotation, playing a crucial role in highly agile and dexterous locomotion. While numerous studies have integrated active spinal joints into quadrupedal robots to enhance agility, most designs simplify control complexity by reducing spinal degrees of freedom (DOF), failing to achieve the spatial tri-axial rotation characteristic of biological spines. Consequently, replicating a multi-DOF biomimetic spine and effectively leveraging it to empower the agile locomotion of quadrupedal robots remains a significant research challenge. In this study, we present S-Cheetah, a quadrupedal robot featuring a 3-DOF bio-inspired serial active spine capable of biomimetic spatial tri-axial rotation. To empower the robot to fully utilize this active spine, we developed a specialized reinforcement learning framework to actively promote the engagement of the introduced spine and maximize the robot's locomotive capabilities by integrating an acceleration curriculum learning strategy with tailored reward functions, such as a gallop gait reward, a spine undulation reward, and a spine steering reward. Experimental results demonstrate that S-Cheetah can achieve a peak speed of 6.9 m/s using the rotary G2 gallop gait and an in-place turning rate of 7.2 rad/s. Besides, the system exhibits an emergent, feline-inspired aerial self-righting capability, allowing it to land stably on four feet from arbitrary orientations during free fall. Finally, through extensive evaluations across diverse locomotion tasks, we prove that the introduction of the proposed 3-DOF spine comprehensively enhances the locomotive agility of quadrupedal robots. Project website: himmy-robotics.github.io/scheetah

</details>


### [129] [A Surveillance Evasion Game with Continuous Sensor Redeployment via Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27917)
*Jaehyeok Kim,Kartik A. Pant,Joseph Kinerson,Kylie Sommer-Kohrt,Worawis Sribunma,Li-Yu Lin,James M. Goppert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于连续传感器重部署的对抗式博弈框架，通过可沿建筑边界滑动的异构传感器网络与双层级优化方法，求解局部纳什均衡以应对无人机对关键基础设施的入侵威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机系统利用传感器周界的时空间隙渗透受限空域，对关键基础设施构成日益严重的威胁。现有博弈方法将防御者限制在离散布局图或固定配置上，无法灵活应对动态攻击。

Method: 将问题建模为二人零和微分博弈，引入沿凸建筑边界连续滑动的传感器重部署技术；采用log-sum-exp光滑逼近保证多边形顶点处的可微性；攻击者最优响应通过STP-RRT*轨迹初始化+非线性规划检测最小化的两步法求解；通过交替双层优化收敛到局部纳什均衡。

Result: 推导了两玩家的一阶驻点条件，建立了可部署的异构传感器放置基线，实现了梯度优化的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提连续传感器重部署框架突破了离散配置的局限性，为反无人机任务中的异构传感器布局提供了理论上可证明收敛性的优化方法。

Abstract: Uncrewed Aerial Systems (UASs) have become a growing threat to the security of critical infrastructure, exploiting spatiotemporal gaps in sensor perimeters to infiltrate restricted airspace undetected. We formulate this interaction as a two-player zero-sum differential game between an adversarial UAS and a heterogeneous sensor network of directional and omnidirectional sensors. Unlike earlier game-theoretic approaches that restrict the defender to discrete placement graphs or fixed configurations, we introduce a continuous sensor redeployment technique in which each sensor slides freely along the convex building boundaries. This is enforced via a log-sum-exp smooth approximation that preserves differentiability at polygon vertices, enabling optimization with gradient-based methods. The attacker's best response is computed via a two-step approach combining STP-RRT* for feasible trajectory initialization and nonlinear programming for detection-minimization refinement. The joint optimization converges to a Local Nash Equilibrium (LNE) via alternating bilevel optimization, with analytical first-order stationarity conditions derived for both players, thereby establishing a deployable baseline for heterogeneous sensor placements in CUAS missions.

</details>


### [130] [Frequency-Guided Action Diffusion via Sub-Frequency Manifold Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27919)
*Junlin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出频率引导算子(FGO)，通过在扩散策略生成过程中逐步扩展频谱带、引导样本经过子频率流形，解决行为克隆中人类演示高频噪声导致策略继承次优行为的问题，在15个机器人操作任务上取得更好平滑性和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然人类演示数据包含间歇性抖动、停顿和动作颤抖等高频率噪声，直接模仿会导致模型继承这些次优行为，且扩散策略的迭代去噪过程会无意放大高频伪影、损失细节。

Method: 提出频率引导算子(FGO)，在扩散策略的生成过程中，通过逐步扩展频谱带，驱动噪声样本经过中间子频率流形，实现隐式频谱操控和平滑动作生成。

Result: 在5个基准的15个机器人操作任务上验证，FGO在增强动作平滑性和时间一致性的同时，保留了任务执行所需细节，取得更优性能。

Conclusion: FGO有效解决了扩散策略在行为克隆中放大高频噪声的问题，为从含噪人类演示中学习平滑、一致的动作策略提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Learning visuomotor policies via behavior cloning typically involves mimicking expert demonstrations collected by human operators. However, natural human demonstrations inherently contain high-frequency noise, such as intermittent jerks, pauses, and action jitter. Training policies to directly imitate these raw trajectories inevitably causes the model to inherit these suboptimal behaviors. This pathology is particularly pronounced in diffusion-based policies, where iterative denoising steps can inadvertently amplify high-frequency artifacts at the expense of meaningful fine-grained details. To address these limitations, we present a novel frequency-based algorithm that enables implicit spectral maneuvering and smooth action generation. Our method, Frequency Guidance Operator (FGO), steers the generation process of diffusion polices by progressively driving the noisy samples through intermediate sub-frequency manifolds with expanding spectral bands. Validated on 15 robotic manipulation tasks from 5 benchmarks, FGO achieves superior performance in enhancing action smoothness and temporal consistency while preserving the details necessary for successful task execution. Project website: https://henrywjl.github.io/frequency-guidance-operator/

</details>


### [131] [SANTS: A State-Adaptive Scheduler for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27947)
*Yirui Sun,Guangyu Zhuge,Keliang Liu,Jie Gu,Xinyu Bing,Zhongxue Gan,Chunxu Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出State-Adaptive Noise Trajectory Scheduler (SANTS)，一种状态自适应噪声轨迹调度器，用于视频到动作的扩散策略。SANTS根据当前视频状态和噪声水平，自适应选择最优去噪深度，避免完全去噪带来的冗余计算。实验表明，SANTS在RoboTwin 2.0上达到94.4%成功率，在真实机器人任务上平均73.1%成功率，同时延迟分别降低81.7%和79.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间的世界动作模型（WAMs）中，完全去噪的视频不一定是最优的动作条件，视频精细化到一定程度后继续去噪可能导致动作误差饱和甚至增加。因此需要一种自适应方法来选择最优的去噪深度。

Method: 提出SANTS：一个轻量级调度器，读取当前视频状态表示和噪声水平，联合预测累积停止风险（cumulative stopping hazard）和相对噪声进度比（relative noise-progression ratio）。采用路径级奖励进行后训练，优化下游动作质量而非中间视频保真度，并显式惩罚冗余的视频状态更新。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0上总体成功率94.4%，七个真实机器人任务平均成功率73.1%，相比完全去噪延迟降低81.7%和79.0%。

Conclusion: 沿视频噪声轨迹的自适应选择可以在保留WAM式未来推理控制优势的同时，大幅减少冗余推理成本。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot manipulation by using video-based future representations to condition action generation. In pixel-space WAMs, however, the best action condition is not necessarily the fully denoised video. Controlled denoising-depth scans show that video refinement can reduce action error up to a state-dependent point, after which the gain may saturate or even reverse when late predictions become less action-relevant or physically unreliable. This suggests that action generation should use a state-dependent point along the video noise trajectory rather than a fixed terminal denoising depth. We introduce State-Adaptive Noise Trajectory Scheduler (SANTS), a lightweight scheduler for video-to-action diffusion policies. At each video decision point, SANTS reads the current video-state representation and noise level, then jointly predicts a cumulative stopping hazard and a relative noise-progression ratio. SANTS is post-trained with a path-level reward computed after the frozen action branch generates the final action chunk, so the scheduler is optimized for downstream action quality rather than intermediate video fidelity, while redundant video-state updates are explicitly penalized. Experiments show that SANTS reaches \(94.4\%\) overall success on RoboTwin 2.0 and \(73.1\%\) average success across seven real-robot tasks, while reducing latency by \(81.7\%\) and \(79.0\%\) relative to full video denoising, respectively. These results indicate that adaptive selection along the video noise trajectory can preserve the control benefits of WAM-style future reasoning while removing much of its redundant inference cost.

</details>


### [132] [VLM-Based Advanced Rider Assistance System for Motorcycle Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27948)
*Mohamed Elnoor,Francesca Baldini,Ananya Trivedi,Faizan M. Tariq,Jovin D'sa,David Isele,Sangjae Bae,Dinesh Manocha,Yosuke Sakamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）和分割检测的摩托车先进骑手辅助系统（ARAS），通过构建密集风险地图实现语义感知和风险感知规划，在CARLA模拟器中相比基线方法取得了更高的成功率和更低的风险暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩托车相比汽车事故风险极高，但先进骑手辅助系统（ARAS）的发展远落后于汽车的高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS），需要开发专门针对摩托车安全性的辅助系统。

Method: 1) 利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行上下文危险推理；2) 结合分割检测构建密集风险地图，编码语义特征（如坑洼严重度、水坑湿滑度）和物理属性（如尺寸、深度）；3) 基于采样的路径规划器，考虑摩托车动力学，推荐油门和转向动作以最小化危险暴露。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器多场景评估中，相比基线方法，该方法实现了更高的任务成功率和更低的风险暴露；定性结果展示了可解释的风险地图和安全的轨迹推荐。

Conclusion: 提出的ARAS系统通过VLM驱动的语义感知和风险地图，有效提升了摩托车在复杂环境中的行驶安全性，且具备可解释性。

Abstract: Motorcycles face disproportionately high crash risks compared to cars due to limited protection and heightened sensitivity to surface hazards, yet Advanced Rider Assistance Systems (ARAS) remain underdeveloped relative to Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS). We propose a novel ARAS that enhances motorcycle safety through semantic perception and risk-aware planning. Our approach leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for contextual hazard reasoning and integrates them with segmentation-based detection to construct dense risk maps. These maps encode both semantic characteristics (e.g., pothole severity, puddle slipperiness) and physical attributes (e.g., size, depth), which produce per-pixel hazard costs that capture motorcycle-specific risks. These maps are used by a sampling-based planner tailored to motorcycle dynamics to recommend throttle and steering actions that minimize hazard exposure while advancing toward the destination. We evaluate our system in different scenarios in the CARLA simulator. Compared to the baseline method, our method achieves higher success rates and lower hazard exposure, while qualitative results demonstrate interpretable risk maps and safe trajectory recommendations.

</details>


### [133] [Simultaneous Contact Selection and Planning for Contact-Rich Manipulation with Cascaded Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27972)
*Zhe Zhang,Xingrong Diao,Haoxiang Liang,Han Yang,Bi-Ke Zhu,Dandan Zhang,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCSP级联优化框架，通过接触选择优化(CSO)和接触规划优化(CPO)实现机器人主动接触位置选择与操作轨迹规划，在复杂操作任务中展现出多样化的操作行为和对不准确动力学及感知噪声的鲁棒控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触隐式方法缺乏自主推理和生成多样化接触位置序列的能力（即主动接触位置选择），限制了其在复杂任务中的适用性。主动接触位置选择面临接触动力学互补性和梯度稀疏等挑战，难以设计统一的接触选择与规划框架。

Method: 提出SCSP级联优化框架，包含两个模块：(1) CSO利用代理接触模型和离散连续优化高效解决接触选择中的非光滑性和耦合问题，实现在线全局搜索最优接触位置；(2) CPO通过先验引导的接触规划，评估CSO产生的参考接触位置并实时生成冗余机械臂的操作轨迹。

Result: 大量仿真和真实实验表明SCSP能产生多样化的操作行为，并在不准确动力学和感知噪声下保持鲁棒控制，且在挑战性操作任务中验证了框架的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCSP成功解决了主动接触位置选择与规划的统一框架问题，通过级联优化方式实现了对复杂接触丰富操作的鲁棒控制，具有较好的泛化性能。

Abstract: We propose an optimization-based framework for robust contact-rich manipulation. Recent contact-implicit methods enable online hybrid planning across contact modes, allowing closed-loop manipulation for a given target state and contact location sequence of the robot and object. However, most existing approaches lack the ability to autonomously reason and generate diverse contact location sequences and manipulation trajectories, i.e., active contact location selection, which limits their applicability to relatively simple tasks. Active contact location selection is challenging due to complementarity in contact dynamics and the sparse gradients, making the design of a unified framework for contact selection and planning difficult. To address these challenges, we introduce Simultaneous Contact Selection and Planning (SCSP), a cascaded optimization framework comprising Contact Selection Optimization (CSO) and Contact Planning Optimization (CPO). CSO leverages a surrogate contact model and discrete-continuous optimization to efficiently resolve the nonsmoothness and coupling in contact selection, enabling online global searching of optimal contact locations. CPO performs prior-guided contact planning by evaluating the reference contact locations produced by CSO and generating corresponding manipulation trajectories in real time for redundant manipulators. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that SCSP produces diverse manipulation behaviors and robust control under inaccurate dynamics and perceptual noise. We further validate the generalization of the framework on challenging manipulation tasks.
  Project website: \href{https://sites.google.com/view/scsp-robot}{https://sites.google.com/view/scsp-robot}.

</details>


### [134] [How Should We Teach Robots? A Comparison of Kinesthetic, Joystick, and Gesture-Based Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28033)
*Petr Vanc,Jan Kristof Behrens,Václav Hlaváč,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过用户研究比较了三种机器人示教方式（动觉引导、摇杆遥控、手势控制），发现动觉引导在复杂任务中表现最好，摇杆在简单任务中占优，手势虽可靠性较低但效果超出预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同示教方式各有优劣，但缺乏对多种方式在相同任务上的系统性对比研究，用户在不同操作条件下的表现和偏好尚不明确。

Method: 招募8名参与者，在三种操作任务中分别使用动觉引导、摇杆遥控和手势控制三种示教方式进行机器人示教，比较回放成功率、改进NASA-TLX工作负荷和常见示教错误。

Result: 动觉引导在方向敏感和接触密集型任务中用时最短、负荷最低、成功率最高；摇杆遥控在简单插销拾取任务中表现最佳；手势控制整体可靠性较低，但在某些任务中达到了与动觉引导相当的效果。

Conclusion: 不同示教方式各有适用场景，动觉引导适合复杂操作任务，摇杆适合简单任务，手势控制虽不稳定但有潜力，未来可根据任务特点选择合适的示教方式。

Abstract: Instructing robots from demonstrations can be done through different teaching modalities, each with different usability and performance trade-offs. This paper compares kinesthetic guidance, joystick teleoperation, and hand gestures in a user study with eight participants. We evaluate replay success, modified NASA-TLX workload, and common teaching errors across three manipulation tasks. Kinesthetic guidance produced the shortest demonstrations, lowest workload, and highest success on the more orientation-sensitive and contact-rich tasks. Joystick teleoperation performed best on simple peg picking. Hand-gesture teaching, although less reliable overall, performed better than expected and in some cases achieved results comparable to kinesthetic guidance.

</details>


### [135] [SAFEVPR: Patch-Based Conformal Verification for Safe Cross-Condition Sequence Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28048)
*Ha Sier,Jiaqiang Zhang,Zhuo Zou,Xianjia Yu,Tomi Westerlund*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFEVPR提出了一种基于保形预测的视觉地点识别安全验证框架，通过互近邻块匹配评分和Mondrian保形校准，在跨条件部署下实现了23/23场景的FDR控制（目标α=0.10），平均接受FDR仅0.014，TPR达0.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列视觉地点识别（VPR）在SLAM和机器人重定位中需要判断top-1候选是否可靠。保形预测虽然适合做接受/拒绝决策，但其有限样本保证依赖于校准数据与测试数据之间的可交换性，这在跨条件部署（如不同季节、天气）下会被违反。现有的VPR方法缺乏在这种分布偏移下提供统计保证的机制。

Method: SAFEVPR是一个无需训练的验证-校准流水线，包含两个核心改进：(1) 用基于冻结DINOv2 ViT特征的互近邻（MNN）块匹配得分替代传统的余弦相似度；(2) 用Mondrian保形Learn-Then-Test校准替代平坦的LTT校准，为不同得分区间分别拟合经Bonferroni校正的阈值。这样在可交换性假设下提供有限样本FDR控制，在条件偏移下则通过实验评估经验有效性。

Result: 在Oxford RobotCar、NCLT和St Lucia数据集的23个跨条件设置上，使用三种冻结VPR骨干网络，SAFEVPR在目标FDR α=0.10下实现了23/23设置的经验有效性，平均接受FDR为0.014，平均TPR为0.75。相比之下，AnyLoc-VLAD和SuperPoint+LightGlue虽然有相当的AUROC，但在相同校准下失败更多。在纹理少、重复性强的场景中，SAFEVPR能安全地弃权而非接受不可靠匹配。

Conclusion: 原始判别能力（如AUROC）不足以保证保形有效性；SAFEVPR的MNN匹配评分结合Mondrian保形校准，能在跨条件VPR中提供可靠的统计保证，实现安全的地点识别决策。

Abstract: Sequence-based visual place recognition (VPR) for SLAM and robot relocalization must decide whether the retrieved top-1 candidate is safe to accept. Conformal prediction is a natural framework for this accept/reject decision, but its finite-sample guarantees rely on exchangeability between calibration and deployment (test) data, which is violated under cross-condition deployment. We introduce SAFEVPR, a non-trainable verification-and-calibration pipeline for safe cross-condition sequence VPR. SAFEVPR replaces the standard backbone cosine similarity with a mutual-nearest-neighbour (MNN) patch-matching score computed from frozen DINOv2 ViT features, and replaces flat Learn-Then-Test calibration with Mondrian conformal LTT, fitting separate Bonferroni-corrected thresholds across score bins. Under exchangeability, these thresholds would provide finite-sample false-discovery-rate (FDR) control; under condition shift, we evaluate empirical validity per deployment. Across 23 cross-condition setups from Oxford RobotCar, NCLT, and St Lucia datasets, using three frozen VPR backbones, SAFEVPR is empirically valid on 23/23 setups at target FDR alpha = 0.10, achieving mean accepted FDR 0.014 and mean true-positive rate (TPR) 0.75. The results show that raw discrimination alone is not sufficient for conformal validity: AnyLoc-VLAD and Super-Point+LightGlue reach comparable area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) but fail more setups under the same calibration. On textureless repetitive scenery, SAFEVPR safely abstains rather than accepting unreliable matches. Code is available at https://github.com/Hasar12139/SafeVPR.

</details>


### [136] [Whose Is This?: Context-Aware Object Ownership Inference with Uncertainty-Guided Questioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28087)
*Saki Hashimoto,Akira Taniguchi,Shoichi Hasegawa,Yoshinobu Hagiwara,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COIN框架，结合大语言模型和共形预测，通过上下文推理和不确定性引导交互来推断机器人情境中的物体所有权，在模拟家庭环境中取得高准确率（子集准确率0.988，平均Jaccard指数0.991）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需要推断物体所有权才能正确理解指令（如"把我的杯子拿给我"），但所有权是隐式属性无法直接观察，现有方法依赖有限线索（如最近使用）在临时共享等场景下不可靠。

Method: 提出COIN框架：1）利用大语言模型（LLM）整合用户背景信息和物体使用历史，评估所有权分数；2）应用共形预测处理不确定性，构建合理所有者集合；3）在预测不确定时选择性生成用户查询。

Result: 在模拟家庭环境中，COIN方法优于所有基线方法，子集准确率达0.988，平均Jaccard指数达0.991；在临时使用和共享所有权场景下仍保持高性能。

Conclusion: 结合上下文推理与不确定性感知交互能同时提升所有权估计的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Service robots must infer object ownership to correctly interpret instructions such as "bring me my cup." However, ownership is a latent attribute that cannot be directly observed, and existing methods often rely on limited cues such as recent usage, making them unreliable in scenarios such as temporary sharing. We propose a framework for context-aware ownership inference with uncertainty-guided interaction (COIN). The method integrates user background information and object usage history using a large language model (LLM) to estimate ownership scores. To handle uncertainty, we apply conformal prediction to construct a set of plausible owners and selectively generate user queries when the prediction is uncertain. Experiments in a simulated home environment show that the proposed method consistently outperforms baseline approaches, achieving a Subset Accuracy of 0.988 and a Mean Jaccard index of 0.991. The method also maintains high performance in scenarios involving temporary use and shared ownership. The results demonstrate that combining contextual reasoning with uncertainty-aware interaction improves both estimation accuracy and robustness. The project page is available at https://emergentsystemlabstudent.github.io/COIN/.

</details>


### [137] [An Operator-Based Approach to STL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28092)
*Panagiotis Rousseas,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可达性值函数算子的信号时序逻辑（STL）新方法，能够处理复杂多嵌套公式，并支持在线控制综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STL验证与控制方法在处理复杂公式和高嵌套度时存在局限性，亟需更高效的框架。

Method: 基于可达性值函数构建算子，直接制定算子级嵌套规则，而非设计STL可达性或控制屏障函数。

Result: 理论上推导了STL公式满足的充要条件，仿真中验证了对复杂片段的处理能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架兼具理论完备性与在线控制实用性，显著提升了STL对复杂嵌套公式的表达力。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL), has recently seen extensive development, owing to its rich expressivenes for autonomous planning and control. Nevertheless, existing verification and control synthesis methods are limited with respect to the complexity and degree of nesting of the formulae. In this work, we propose a novel approach to STL based on an operator acting on reachability value functions. This constitutes a new theoretical framework for handling complex multi-nested formulae while at the same time providing tools for on-line control synthesis. In contrast to focusing on the design of STL-based reachability (or control barrier) functions, we develop operator-based nesting rules directly. Our method's expressiveness is demonstrated both theoretically, where necessary and sufficient conditions for STL formula satisfaction are extracted, as well as in simulations with complex fragments.

</details>


### [138] [ICAN-Deploy: Identity-Stable Canary Deployment for Safety-Critical Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28097)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICAN-Deploy，一种在金丝雀部署中保持加密身份哈希不变性的中间件，使得安全关键的具身智能体无需在每个金丝雀周期重新认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金丝雀部署控制器（Argo Rollouts、Spinnaker、Flagger）在部署过程中会改变系统的加密身份，这对无状态微服务无害，但对安全关键的具身智能体而言，打破了"你认证的智能体仍是原来那个"的承诺，导致每次金丝雀部署都需要重新认证。

Method: 通过状态机分离能力名称（冻结、哈希）和能力版本（可变的运行时状态），在能力名+版本的外壳内保持身份哈希不变量；在LLM驱动机器人的运行时治理层实现该中间件。

Result: 通过封闭式证明、AST lint和TLA+模型检验验证不变性；在MuJoCo中Franka Panda机械臂上进行了N=100次真实金丝雀周期，验证零漂移，入口延迟95% BCa CI [1.52, 2.01] ms；而将版本折叠进清单的特征标记稻草人在相同工作负载上伪造不变性。

Conclusion: 在身份创建时一次性认证的系统，可以在同一认证下，在版本与名称的外壳内，交付任意能力演化。

Abstract: Canary deployment routes a fraction of traffic to a new software version, monitors metrics, and rolls back on regression. Mainstream controllers (Argo Rollouts, Spinnaker, Flagger) change the deployed system's cryptographic identity during the canary window. The drift is harmless for stateless microservices but breaks the claim that "the agent you certified is still the agent you have" for safety-critical embodied agents, forcing re-certification per canary. We present ICAN-Deploy (Identity-stable CANary Deployment), a middleware construction whose state machine holds the identity hash invariant across the canary window by separating capability names (frozen, hashed) from capability versions (mutable runtime state). We implement ICAN-Deploy inside a runtime governance layer for LLM-driven robots and verify invariance by closed-form proof, AST lint, and TLA+ model-checking, then corroborate over N=100 real canary cycles on a Franka Panda arm in MuJoCo (zero drift; entry latency 95% BCa CI [1.52, 2.01] ms). A feature-flagged strawman that folds versions into the manifest falsifies on the same workload. A system certified once at identity-creation time can then ship arbitrary capability evolution under that same certification, within the version-and-name envelope.

</details>


### [139] [STR Robot: Design of an Autonomous Mobile Robot from Simulation to Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28110)
*Vinh Nguyen,Gia-Uy Le,Tien-Dat Nguyen,Tri-Tin Nguyen,Vinh-Hao Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套从仿真到真实环境部署的自主移动机器人框架，重点开发了机载控制、自定位和自主导航系统，并在仿真验证后成功应用于真实机器人，证明了仿真对开发可靠机器人系统的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用仿真工具提高自主机器人系统开发与验证效率，在现有机械平台上实现完整的仿真到真实部署流程。

Method: 基于现有机械平台，开发机载控制、自定位和自主导航系统；先在仿真环境中完成整体框架的开发和测试，再部署到真实机器人上进行实验评估。

Result: 所提方法具备可行性，仿真环境为开发可靠的自主移动机器人系统提供了有效基础。

Conclusion: 仿真到真实的迁移策略可行，仿真能够有效支撑自主移动机器人系统的可靠开发。

Abstract: With the rapid development of simulation tools, the development and validation of autonomous robotic systems have become more efficient before real-world deployment. This paper presents a simulation-to-real implementation of an autonomous mobile robot based on an existing mechanical platform. Instead of focusing on mechanical design, our work concentrates on the development of the onboard control, self-localization, and autonomous navigation system. The proposed robot is equipped with onboard sensing and computation to estimate its pose and navigate autonomously in the environment. The overall framework is first developed and tested in simulation, and then deployed on the real robot for experimental evaluation. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and show that simulation provides an effective foundation for developing reliable autonomous mobile robot systems. The source code will be released at https://ntdathp.github.io/outdoor-robot-web.

</details>


### [140] [Provably Guaranteed Polytopic Uncertainty Quantification for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28172)
*Guangyang Zeng,Yulong Gao,Yuan Shen,Lingpeng Chen,Haoying Li,Guodong Shi,Junfeng Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于多面体表示的3D-3D地标SLAM中具有理论保证的不确定性量化算法，包含前向UQ（建图）、后向UQ（位姿追踪）和位姿复合三个模块，并结合保形预测提升实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键型机器人应用中，感知过程需要具有保障且实用的不确定性量化，而现有工作要么缺乏形式化包含保证，要么依赖严格建模假设，或仅关注位姿估计而非完整SLAM流程。

Method: 提出三个基础UQ模块：前向UQ（建图）、后向UQ（位姿追踪）和位姿复合；采用多面体表示不确定性集合以实现可追踪计算和统一处理；结合保形预测从数据中校准测量不确定性。

Result: 仿真和实验表明所提算法既提供强理论保证，又具备实际可用性。

Conclusion: 本文为SLAM中的不确定性量化提供了具有确定性保证且实用的解决方案，开源代码可复现和推广。

Abstract: In safety-critical robotics applications, guaranteed and practical uncertainty quantification (UQ) in perception is vital. Many existing works either offer no formal containment guarantee, rely on restrictive modeling assumptions, or focus only on pose estimation rather than a complete SLAM pipeline. This paper presents provably guaranteed UQ algorithms for 3D-3D landmark-based SLAM. The algorithms consist of three basic UQ modules: forward UQ for mapping, backward UQ for pose tracking, and pose compound. Each module produces a certified uncertainty set; when the input uncertainty bounds are deterministic, the output sets inherit deterministic guarantees, i.e., they provably contain the true poses and landmarks. Specifically, we use polytopes to represent uncertainty sets, enabling tractable computations and a unified treatment of pose uncertainty. To enhance algorithms' practical usability, we incorporate conformal prediction to calibrate measurement uncertainty from data with prescribed probability. Simulations and experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms provide both strong theoretical guarantees and practical usability. The code is open-sourced at https://github.com/LIAS-CUHKSZ/Polytopic-SLAM-Uncertainty-Quantification.

</details>


### [141] [Visualizing Latent Phase Structures in Locomotion Policies: A Multi-Environment Study with Temporal Feature Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28186)
*Daisuke Yasui,Toshitaka Matuki,Hiroshi Sato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种从深度强化学习训练的运动控制策略中挖掘潜在运动阶段结构的框架，通过扩展聚类特征并引入抑制自转移的聚类数确定方法，在Ant、HalfCheetah和Walker2D环境中识别出比现有方法更清晰规则的运动阶段结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在运动控制任务中表现优异，但训练后的策略网络内部获得的运动结构难以可视化，而已知生物力学中运动控制通过重复的运动阶段（如支撑相和摆动相）实现，因此需要一种方法来揭示这些潜在结构。

Method: 将聚类特征从仅状态观测扩展到包含动作、下一状态和下一动作的增强特征，并引入一种通过抑制自转移来确定聚类数量的方法。

Result: 在Ant-v5、HalfCheetah-v5和Walker2D-v5三个环境中，该方法成功识别出比现有方法更清晰、更规则的运动阶段结构。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够有效从DRL训练的运动控制策略中挖掘出具有清晰转移规则的运动阶段结构，有助于理解深度神经网络内部获得的运动表征。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has been shown to achieve high performance on locomotion control tasks in MuJoCo benchmarks such as HalfCheetah, Ant, and Walker2D. However, visualizing the motion structures internally obtained by a trained policy function implemented as a deep neural network remains challenging. It is known from biomechanics and related fields that locomotion control is realized through the repetition of motion phases such as the stance phase and swing phase. In this study, we propose a framework for uncovering latent motion phase structures from trajectories generated by locomotion control policies through interaction with the environment. The proposed method extends the clustering features from state observations alone to augmented features including actions, next states, and next actions, and introduces a method for determining the number of clusters that suppresses self-transitions. Applying the proposed method to three environments -- Ant-v5, HalfCheetah-v5, and Walker2D-v5 -- we successfully identified phase structures with clearer and more regular transition rules than those obtained by the existing method.

</details>


### [142] [Natural Functional Gradients for Smooth Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28202)
*Kisang Park,Chanwoo Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自然函数梯度的轨迹优化框架，通过在函数空间中进行几何感知更新，生成无碰撞且平滑的机器人运动轨迹，在狭窄环境和高约束任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境和狭窄通道中，可行区域高度受限且碎片化，生成无碰撞且平滑的运动轨迹仍是机器人操作中的核心挑战。

Method: 在函数空间中利用自然函数梯度进行几何感知更新，优化高斯平滑的替代目标函数（通过平滑轨迹扰动正则化优化景观），推导出仅需黑盒轨迹评估的蒙特卡洛自然函数梯度估计器。

Result: 在约束机器人操作任务中，该方法比代表性的规划和轨迹优化基线方法生成了更可行、更平滑的运动，尤其在窄几何间隙环境中表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了轨迹规则性与时间离散化的解耦，在解析梯度不可用或不可靠（如碰撞检测、接触丰富的仿真）时仍有效，为约束环境下的轨迹优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generating collision-free and smooth motions remains a central challenge in robotic manipulation, particularly in cluttered environments and narrow passages where feasible regions are highly constrained and fragmented. We propose a trajectory optimization framework that performs geometry-aware updates directly in function space using natural functional gradients. The method optimizes a Gaussian-smoothed surrogate objective that regularizes the optimization landscape through smooth trajectory perturbations while preserving trajectory-level structure. Because the updates are defined intrinsically in function space, trajectory regularity can be controlled independently of a particular time discretization. We derive a practical Monte-Carlo estimator of the natural functional gradient that requires only black-box trajectory evaluations, making the method applicable when analytic gradients are unavailable or unreliable due to collision checking and contact-rich simulation. Experiments on constrained robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method improves trajectory feasibility and produces smoother motions than representative planning and trajectory optimization baselines in environments with narrow geometric clearances. Additional results, videos, and implementation details are available at the project page: https://kisangpark.github.io/natural-functional-gradient/

</details>


### [143] [ProgVLA: Progress-Aware Robot Manipulation Skill Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28231)
*Seungsu Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Seungmin Baek,Jean-Michel Renders*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProgVLA是一个紧凑的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过两阶段Perceiver重采样压缩多模态序列，并引入基于离线强化学习的进度头来估计任务进度，在0.1B参数量下达到与大型预训练基线竞争甚至超越的性能，尤其在长程和困难任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算和内存预算有限的条件下，现有VLA模型处理长多模态序列时效率低下，尤其缺乏对任务进度的显式建模，导致长程操作任务表现不佳。

Method: 1) 两阶段Perceiver重采样编码器，将变长多模态流压缩为固定长度的控制上下文令牌；2) 基于离线强化学习的进度头，学习对归一化剩余时域目标的评论，提供内部任务进度估计，并实现优势/成功加权的流匹配模仿学习。

Result: 0.1B参数的ProgVLA在多个多任务操作基准上达到与大型预训练基线竞争的性能，在长程和困难任务上超越它们。消融实验表明上下文重采样器和任务自适应视觉微调贡献最大，进度感知训练在长程和多物体任务上提供一致增益。

Conclusion: ProgVLA证明了紧凑型VLA模型通过显式进度建模和高效多模态压缩，能够在资源受限条件下实现可靠的机器人操作，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present ProgVLA, a compact vision-language-action (VLA) model designed for reliable robot manipulation under tight compute and memory budgets. The model specifically focuses on efficiently processing long multi-modal sequences by maintaining an explicit representation of task progress over extended horizons. To this end, ProgVLA integrates two key components. First, a multi-modal encoder with a two-stage Perceiver resampling scheme compresses variable-length visual, language, and proprioceptive streams into a fixed set of control-ready context tokens, substantially reducing sequence length while preserving cross-modal grounding. Second, an auxiliary set of progress heads is trained with offline reinforcement learning (RL) objectives to jointly learn critics over normalized remaining-horizon targets. This provides the policy with an internal estimate of task progress and enables advantage- and success-weighted flow-matching imitation learning. On two well-established multi-task robot manipulation benchmarks, a 0.1B-parameter ProgVLA model reaches success rates that are competitive with, and on long-horizon and harder task tiers exceed, substantially larger pretrained baselines. Ablations indicate that the learned context resampler and task-adaptive visual fine-tuning are the largest single contributors, while progress-aware training provides a consistent additional gain that is concentrated on long-horizon and multi-object tasks. We further validate the approach in real-world toy-kitchen environments.

</details>


### [144] [POINav: Benchmarking and Enhancing Final-Meters Arrival in Real-World Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28237)
*Ruiyan Gong,Meisheng Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Mingchao Sun,Yanfen Shen,Zedong Chu,Zhining Gu,Wei Guo,Xiaolong Cheng,Qiming Li,Kangning Niu,Yanqing Zhu,Xiaolong Wu,Tianlun Li,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了POINav-Bench，首个面向真实世界POI目标导航的闭环评估基准，包含11个商业区域、126,398㎡和163个POI，并提出了POINav Brain-Action框架，结合70K真实世界标识-入口数据集，有效解决导航"最后几米"难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准在POI目标导航中面临粒度粗或模拟-真实差距大的问题，缺乏对真实世界封闭式POI导航的高保真评估平台。

Method: 构建基于3D高斯泼溅重建的真实商业区基准（POINav-Bench），提出Brain-Action双模块框架：Brain模块进行POI接地推理，Action模块预测连续航点；同时构建70K真实标识-入口数据集。

Result: 覆盖11个商业区、126,398㎡、163个POI；70K真实世界标识-入口对；实验验证框架在真实POI导航中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为改进真实世界POI目标导航提供了可行路径，填补了该领域基准和方法的空白。

Abstract: Real-world navigation is fundamentally driven by Points of Interest (POIs), yet reaching a precise POI remains a critical "final-meters" challenge. Existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks of POI-goal navigation often suffer from coarse granularity or significant sim-to-real gaps due to generated scene. To bridge this gap, we present POINav-Bench, the first benchmark designed for closed-loop evaluation of real-world POI-goal navigation. It comprises 11 commercial areas reconstructed from real-world captures using 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), covering 126,398 $m^{2}$ in total and spanning 163 distinct POIs. With traversability-aware annotations and reference trajectories, POINav-Bench enables high-fidelity evaluation of navigation agents in realistic, POI-rich real-world environments. Building on this, we propose the POINav Brain-Action Framework where a Brain module performs POI-grounded reasoning to guide an Action module in predicting continuous waypoints for real-world execution. We further curate the POINav-Dataset, containing 70K real-world signage-entrance pairs. Experiments show that our framework provides a viable path toward refining real-world POI-goal navigation.

</details>


### [145] [Natural Locomotion: Principle and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28254)
*Mirado Mortel,Luc Jaulin,Lionel Lapierre,Simon Rohou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自然运动作为一种交换原理：当内部振荡器周期性回返、身体位姿漂移、且平均推进-振荡器交换功率（POE功率）在一个周期内为零时，运动是自然的。通过闭/开构造方法，在非完整约束系统（如Chaplygin雪橇车）上验证该原理，并提出了自然运动流形（NLM）家族的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人运动依赖预定轨迹控制，效率较低。作者希望利用被动动力学、柔顺性和共振等机制，从原理层面定义"自然运动"，使机器人 locomotion 更高效。

Method: 提出"自然运动流形（NLM）"框架，采用闭/开构造方法：先封闭推进通道得到有效内部振荡器（用作用-角变量或非线性模态描述），再重新打开通道重建位姿，筛选满足内部回返和零平均POE功率的循环。

Result: 在Chaplygin雪橇/摆驱动车和三维延伸体两个理想非完整无滑移系统上验证。标量情况下，POE闭包等价于缺失的内部回返条件，得到定理支持的NLM计算；多自由度情况下，POE闭包仍需补充模态身份、内部回返、动力学一致性等条件。

Conclusion: 自然运动转为一个设计问题：哪些被动架构支持零个、一个或多个经过认证的NLM家族？该原理为高效机器人设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Robotic locomotion can become efficient when mechanisms exploit passive dynamics, compliance, and resonance rather than track prescribed trajectories. This paper formulates natural locomotion as an exchange principle for systems whose motion is mediated by environmental constraints or interactions. A motion is natural when an internal oscillator returns periodically, the body pose drifts, and the mean Propulsion--Oscillator Exchange power (POE power) vanishes over one cycle. The selected family is a Natural Locomotion Manifold (NLM). We develop the conservative realization of this principle for continuous ideal environmental constraints: the constraints do no external work, total mechanical energy is conserved, and zero mean POE power is an internal exchange with the environment-mediated propulsive channel, not external energy input.
  The method is a closed/open construction. The propulsive channel is first closed to reveal an effective internal oscillator, organized by scalar action-angle structure in one effective degree of freedom or by nonlinear modal sectors in several degrees of freedom. The channel is then reopened, pose is reconstructed, and accepted cycles must preserve internal recurrence and zero mean POE power.
  We demonstrate the principle on two ideal nonholonomic no-slip systems: a Chaplygin-sleigh / pendulum-driven car and a three-body extension. In the scalar case, POE closure is equivalent to the missing internal return condition, giving a theorem-backed computation of the NLM family. In the multi-degree case, POE closure remains necessary but must be completed by modal identity, internal return, dynamics consistency, same fixed passive architecture, and nonzero displacement. Natural locomotion becomes a design question: which passive architectures support no, one, or several certified NLM families?

</details>


### [146] [IMU Propagation as Preintegration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28279)
*Jianzhu Huai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明IMU预积分与IMU传播在数学上是等价的，并提出了一种与具体约定无关的统一视角，使得现有传播代码可直接用于预积分计算，反之亦然。


<details>
